SQLite is the live database — all reads and writes go through it. `issues.jsonl` is a git-synced artifact exported on demand: one entry per bead, committed to the repo for backup, sharing, and recovery. Concurrent claiming is handled by SQLite's `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` transaction — no server, no daemon, no flock required.\n\n## Core Principles\n\n1. **SQLite is the live store** — all mutations are SQL writes; reads are SQL queries. Fast, concurrent-read, serialized-write via WAL mode.\n2. **JSONL is the git artifact** — exported on `bf sync`, committed to git, one entry per bead. Used for backup, cross-machine sharing, and recovery. Never written during normal operation.\n3. **Drop-in CLI** — all `br` commands work under `bf` with the same flags and output formats\n4. **`BEGIN IMMEDIATE` for atomic claim** — the read-score-update sequence runs inside a single SQLite write transaction, eliminating the race condition `br` has without requiring flock or a daemon\n\n---\n\n## Phase 1: Core Library — Data Model & JSONL Import/Export\n\n### 1.1 Data Model (`src/model.rs`)\n\nPort the exact `Issue`, `Status`, `Priority`, `IssueType`, `Dependency`, `DependencyType`, `Comment`, and `Event` structs from beads_rust with identical Serde attributes. This is the wire format — every field, every `skip_serializing_if`, every rename must match.\n\nKey structs and their JSONL serialization:\n\n```\nStatus:       open | in_progress | blocked | deferred | draft | closed | tombstone | pinned | Custom(String)\nPriority:     transparent i32 (0=Critical, 4=Backlog)\nIssueType:    task | bug | feature | epic | chore | docs | question | Custom(String)\nDependency:   { issue_id, depends_on_id, type (renamed from dep_type), created_at, created_by?, metadata?, thread_id? }\nComment:      { id: i64, issue_id, author, text (renamed from body), created_at }\nEvent:        { id: i64, issue_id, event_type, actor, old_value?, new_value?, comment?, created_at }\n```\n\nIssue fields (alphabetical by JSON key):\n```\nid, title, description?, design?, acceptance_criteria?, notes?,\nstatus, priority, issue_type, assignee?, owner?, estimated_minutes?,\ncreated_at, created_by?, updated_at, closed_at?, close_reason?,\nclosed_by_session?, due_at?, defer_until?, external_ref?,\nsource_system?, source_repo, deleted_at?, deleted_by?, delete_reason?,\noriginal_type?, compaction_level (default 0), compacted_at?,\ncompacted_at_commit?, original_size?, sender?, ephemeral (default false),\npinned (default false), is_template (default false),\nlabels: Vec<String>, dependencies: Vec<Dependency>, comments: Vec<Comment>\n```\n\n### 1.2 JSONL Import (`src/jsonl.rs`)\n\nJSONL is read only during `bf sync --import` (pulling state from git) and `bf doctor --repair` (rebuilding SQLite from the artifact). Never read during normal CLI operation.\n\n```rust\nfn import_jsonl(jsonl_path: &Path, storage: &Storage) -> Result<ImportResult> {\n    let file = File::open(jsonl_path)?;\n    let reader = BufReader::new(file);\n    storage.with_write_transaction(|tx| {\n        for line in reader.lines() {\n            let issue: Issue = serde_json::from_str(&line?)?;\n            tx.upsert_issue(&issue)?;\n        }\n        Ok(())\n    })\n}\n```\n\nFunctions:\n- `import_jsonl(path, storage)` — bulk upsert from JSONL into SQLite\n- `stream_issues(path) -> impl Iterator<Item = Result<Issue>>` — lazy line iterator (used by import and doctor)\n\n### 1.3 JSONL Export (`src/jsonl.rs`)\n\nJSONL is written only during `bf sync --flush` (preparing the git artifact). Exports current bead state from SQLite, one entry per bead, sorted by ID for stable diffs. Atomic temp+rename.\n\n```rust\nfn export_jsonl(jsonl_path: &Path, storage: &Storage) -> Result<ExportResult> {\n    let issues = storage.list_all_issues()?;  // sorted by ID\n    let temp_path = jsonl_path.with_extension(\"jsonl.tmp\");\n    let mut writer = BufWriter::new(File::create_new(&temp_path)?);\n    for issue in &issues {\n        serde_json::to_writer(&mut writer, issue)?;\n        writer.write_all(b\"\\n\")?;\n    }\n    writer.flush()?;\n    drop(writer);\n    fs::rename(&temp_path, jsonl_path)?;\n    Ok(ExportResult { count: issues.len() })\n}\n```\n\nFunctions:\n- `export_jsonl(path, storage)` — dump all beads from SQLite to JSONL (atomic)\n- `export_jsonl_dirty(path, storage)` — export only dirty beads (incremental, faster for large workspaces)\n\n### 1.4 Config (`src/config.rs`)\n\nParse `.beads/config.yaml` with the same fields br uses:\n\n```yaml\nissue_prefixes: [bd, fg]\ndefault_priority: 2","numLines":100,"startLine":1,"totalLines":1560}}}

},\n470\t}\n471\t\n472\t#[derive(Subcommand)]\n473\tpub enum DepCommands {\n474\t    /// Add a dependency\n475\t    Add {\n476\t        /// Issue ID (the one that will depend on something)\n477\t },\n623\t}\n624\t\n625\tpub fn run_cli() -> Result<Cli> {\n626\t    Ok(Cli::try_parse()?)\n627\t}\n628\t\n629\tpub fn run(cli: Cli) -> Result<()> {\n630\t    let workspace = cli.workspace.unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from(\".\"));\n631\t\n632\t    // For init command, we allow the .beads directory to not exist yet\n633\t    match &cli.command {\n634\t        Commands::Init { .. } => {\n635\t            let beads_dir = workspace.join(\".beads\");\n636\t            return match cli.command {\n637\t                Commands::Init { prefix } => cmd_init(&beads_dir, &prefix),\n638\t                _ => unreachable!(),\n639\t            };\n640\t        }\n641\t        _ => {}\n642\t    }\n643\t\n644\t    let beads_dir = find_beads_dir(&workspace)\n645\t        .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!(\"No .beads directory found in {:?}\", workspace))?;\n646\t\n647\t    match cli.command {\n648\t        Commands::Init { prefix } => cmd_init(&beads_dir, &prefix),\n649\t        Commands::Create { title, type_, priority, description, assignee, label } => {\n650\t            cmd_create(&beads_dir, title, type_, priority, description, assignee, label)\n651\t        }\n652\t        Commands::List { status, type_, assignee, priority, limit, all, format, json } => {\n653\t            let format = if json { \"json\".to_string() } else { format };\n654\t            cmd_list(&beads_dir, status, type_, assignee, priority, limit, all, &format)\n655\t        }\n656\t        Commands::Show { id, format, json } => {\n657\t            let format = if json { \"json\".to_string() } else { format };\n658\t            cmd_show(&beads_dir, &id, &format)\n659\t        }\n660\t        Commands::Update { id, title, status, priority, assignee } => {\n661\t            cmd_update(&beads_dir, &id, title, status, priority, assignee)\n662\t        }\n663\t        Commands::Close { id, reason } => cmd_close(&beads_dir, &id, &reason),\n664\t        Commands::Reopen { id } => cmd_reopen(&beads_dir, &id),\n665\t        Commands::Delete { id } => cmd_delete(&beads_dir, &id),\n666\t        Commands::Ready { limit, format, json } => {\n667\t            let format = if json { \"json\".to_string() } else { format };\n668\t            cmd_ready(&beads_dir, limit, &format)\n669\t        }\n670\t        Commands::Claim { assignee, model, harness, harness_version, any, fallback, workspace_paths, dry_run, format, json } => {\n671\t            let format = if json { \"json\".to_string() } else { format };\n672\t            cmd_claim(&beads_dir, &assignee, model, harness, harness_version, any, fallback, &workspace_paths, dry_run, &format)\n673\t        }\n674\t        Commands::Sync { flush_only, import_only } => cmd_sync(&beads_dir, flush_only, import_only),\n675\t        Commands::Doctor { repair } => cmd_doctor(&beads_dir, repair),\n676\t        Commands::Count { status } => cmd_count(&beads_dir, status),\n677\t        Commands::Batch { file, json, stdin } => cmd_batch(&beads_dir, file, json, stdin),\n678\t        Commands::Mitosis { id, children, reason, format } => cmd_mitosis(&beads_dir, &id, &children, &reason, &format),\n679\t        Commands::Dep(dep) => cmd_dep(&beads_dir, dep),\n680\t        Commands::Label(label) => cmd_label(&beads_dir, label),\n681\t        Commands::Comments(comments) => cmd_comments(&beads_dir, comments),\n682\t        Commands::Search { query, status, type_, assignee, label, priority_min, priority_max, limit, format } => {\n683\t            cmd_search(&beads_dir, query, status, type_, assignee, label, priority_min, priority_max, limit, &format)\n684\t        }\n685\t        Commands::Stats { by_type, by_priority, by_assignee, by_label, format } => {\n686\t            cmd_stats(&beads_dir, by_type, by_priority, by_assignee, by_label, &format)\n687\t        }\n688\t        Commands::Schema { target, format } => cmd_schema(&target, &format),\n689\t        Commands::Config(config) => cmd_config(&beads_dir, config),\n690\t        Commands::Velocity { model, harness, format } => cmd_velocity(&beads_dir, model, harness, &format),\n691\t        Commands::Labels { id, format } => cmd_labels(&beads_dir, &id, &format),\n692\t        Commands::Annotate(annotate) => cmd_annotate(&beads_dir, annotate),\n693\t        Commands::Log { id, limit, since, actor, status_changes, diff, format, json } => {\n694\t            let format = if json { \"json\".to_string() } else { format };\n695\t            cmd_log(&beads_dir, id, limit, since, actor, status_changes, diff, &format)\n696\t        }\n697\t        Commands::CriticalPath { id, max_depth, format } => cmd_critical_path(&beads_dir, &id, max_depth, &format),\n698\t        Commands::Rotate { days, dry_run } => cmd_rotate(&beads_dir, days, dry_run),\n699\t        Commands::Migrate { workspace, from_jsonl, seed_velocity, dry_run, skip_verify } => {\n700\t            cmd_migrate(&beads_dir, workspace, from_jsonl, seed_velocity, dry_run, skip_verify)\n701\t        }\n702\t    }\n703\t}\n704\t\n705\tfn cmd_init(beads_dir: &PathBuf, prefix: &str) -> Result<()> {\n706\t    std::fs::create_dir_all(beads_dir)?;\n707\t\n708\t    let config_path = beads_dir.join(\"config.yaml\");\n709\t    if !config_path.exists() {\n710\t        let config = format!(\n711\t            r#\"issue_prefixes: [{}]\n712\tdefault_priority: 2\n713\tdefault_type: task\n714\tclaim_ttl_minutes: 30\n715\t\"#,\n716\t            prefix\n717\t        );\n718\t        std::fs::write(&config_path, config)?;\n719\t    }\n720\t\n721\t    let metadata_path = beads_dir.join(\"metadata.json\");\n722\t    if !metadata_path.exists() {\n723\t        let metadata = r#\"{\"database\": \"beads.db\", \"jsonl_export\": \"issues.jsonl\"}\"#;\n724\t        std::fs::write(&metadata_path, metadata)?;\n725\t    }\n726\t\n727\t    let db_path = beads_dir.join(\"beads.db\");\n728\t    let _storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n729\t\n730\t    let gitignore_path = beads_dir.join(\".gitignore\");\n731\t    if !gitignore_path.exists() {\n732\t        std::fs::write(&gitignore_path, \"beads.db\\nbeads.db-shm\\nbeads.db-wal\\n\")?;\n733\t    }\n734\t\n735\t    println!(\"Initialized bead-forge workspace in {:?}\", beads_dir);\n736\t    Ok(())\n737\t}\n738\t\n739\tfn cmd_create(\n740\t    beads_dir: &PathBuf,\n741\t    title: String,\n742\t    type_: String,\n743\t    priority: i32,\n744\t    description: Option<String>,\n745\t    assignee: Option<String>,\n746\t    labels: Vec<String>,\n747\t) -> Result<()> {\n748\t    let config = load_config(beads_dir)?;\n749\t    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n750\t    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n751\t    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n752\t\n753\t    let count = storage.count_issues()?;\n754\t    let prefix = get_default_prefix(&config);\n755\t    let id = crate::id::generate_id(prefix, count);\n756\t\n757\t    let mut issue = Issue::new(id.clone(), title, \".\".to_string());\n758\t    issue.issue_type = IssueType::from_str(type_.as_str()).map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?;\n759\t    issue.priority = Priority(priority);\n760\t    issue.description = description;\n761\t    issue.assignee = assignee;\n762\t    issue.labels = labels;\n763\t\n764\t    storage.create_issue(&issue)?;\n765\t\n766\t    println!(\"{}\", id);\n767\t    Ok(())\n768\t}\n769\t\n770\tfn cmd_list(\n771\t    beads_dir: &PathBuf,\n772\t    status: Option<String>,\n773\t    type_: Option<String>,\n774\t    assignee: Option<String>,\n775\t    priority: Option<i32>,\n776\t    limit: Option<usize>,\n777\t    all: bool,\n778\t    format: &str,\n779\t) -> Result<()> {\n780\t    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n781\t    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n782\t    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n783\t\n784\t    let mut issues = if all {\n785\t        // Include archived beads\n786\t        list_all_with_archives(beads_dir)?\n787\t    } else {\n788\t        // Only from database\n789\t        let mut filter = IssueFilter::default();\n790\t        if let Some(ref s) = status {\n791\t            filter.status = Some(Status::from_str(s.as_str()).map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?);\n792\t        }\n793\t        if let Some(ref t) = type_ {\n794\t            filter.issue_type = Some(IssueType::from_str(t.as_str()).map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?);\n795\t        }\n796\t        filter.assignee = assignee.clone();\n797\t        filter.priority = priority;\n798\t        // --limit 0 means unlimited\n799\t        filter.limit = limit.and_then(|l| if l == 0 { None } else { Some(l) });\n800\t        storage.list_issues(&filter)?\n801\t    };\n802\t\n803\t    // Apply additional filters for --all mode (since we're not using DB filters)\n804\t    if all {\n805\t        if let Some(ref s) = status {\n806\t            let status_filter = Status::from_str(s.as_str()).map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?;\n807\t            issues.retain(|i| i.status == status_filter);\n808\t        }\n809\t        if let Some(ref t) = type_ {\n810\t            let type_filter = IssueType::from_str(t.as_str()).map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?;\n811\t            issues.retain(|i| i.issue_type == type_filter);\n812\t        }\n813\t        if let Some(ref assignee_val) = assignee {\n814\t            issues.retain(|i| i.assignee.as_deref() == Some(assignee_val));\n815\t        }\n816\t        if let Some(p) = priority {\n817\t            issues.retain(|i| i.priority.0 == p);\n818\t        }\n819\t        // Apply limit\n820\t        if let Some(l) = limit {\n821\t            if l != 0 {\n822\t                issues.truncate(l);\n823\t            }\n824\t        }\n825\t    }\n826\t\n827\t    let output_format = OutputFormat::from_str(format).unwrap_or(OutputFormat::Text);\n828\t    let formatter = get_formatter(output_format);\n829\t    print!(\"{}\", formatter.format_issues(&issues));\n830\t\n831\t    Ok(())\n832\t}\n833\t\n834\tfn cmd_show(beads_dir: &PathBuf, id: &str, format: &str) -> Result<()> {\n835\t    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n836\t    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n837\t    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n838\t\n839\t    let issue = match storage.get_issue(id)? {\n840\t        Some(i) => i,\n841\t        None => {\n842\t            // Search archives\n843\t            find_bead_in_archives(beads_dir, id)?\n844\t                .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!(\"Bead not found: {}\", id))?\n845\t        }\n846\t    };\n847\t\n848\t    match format {\n849\t        \"json\" => {\n850\t            // Strip dependencies/comments before serializing: NEEDLE's BrDependency\n851\t            // format ({id, title, status, priority, dependency_type}) differs from\n852\t            // bead-forge's Dependency format ({issue_id, depends_on_id, type, ...}).\n853\t            // NEEDLE has #[serde(default)] on the deps field so empty is fine.\n854\t            let mut out = issue;\n855\t            out.dependencies = vec![];\n856\t            out.comments = vec![];\n857\t            // Wrap in array so NEEDLE's parse_single_bead (Vec<Bead> → first) works.\n858\t            println!(\"{}\", serde_json::to_string(&vec![out])?);\n859\t        }\n860\t        \"toon\" => {\n861\t            println!(\"ID: {}\", issue.id);\n862\t            println!(\"Title: {}\", issue.title);\n863\t            println!(\"Status: {}\", issue.status);\n864\t            println!(\"Priority: {}\", issue.priority);\n865\t            println!(\"Type: {}\", issue.issue_type);\n866\t            if let Some(desc) = &issue.description {\n867\t                println!(\"Description: {}\", desc);\n868\t            }\n869\t #[arg(long, default_value = \"text\")]\n        format: String,\n\n        /// Output JSON (alias for --format json)\n        #[arg(long)]\n        json: bool,\n    },\n\n    /// Show bead details\n    Show {\n        /// Bead ID\n        id: String,\n\n        /// Output format (text, json, toon)\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"text\")]\n        format: String,\n\n        /// Output JSON (alias for --format json)\n        #[arg(long)]\n        json: bool,\n    },\n\n    /// Update a bead\n    Update {\n        /// Bead ID\n        id: String,\n\n        /// New title\n        #[arg(long)]\n        title: Option<String>,\n\n        /// New status\n        #[arg(long)]\n        status: Option<String>,\n\n        /// New priority\n        #[arg(long)]\n        priority: Option<i32>,\n\n        /// New assignee\n        #[arg(long)]\n        assignee: Option<String>,\n    },\n\n    /// Close a bead\n    Close {\n        /// Bead ID\n        id: String,\n\n        /// Close reason\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"Completed\")]\n        reason: String,\n    },\n\n    /// Reopen a bead\n    Reopen {\n        /// Bead ID\n        id: String,\n    },\n\n    /// Delete a bead\n    Delete {\n        /// Bead ID\n        id: String,\n    },\n\n    /// Show ready (unblocked) beads\n    Ready {\n        /// Limit results (0 = unlimited)\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"10\")]\n        limit: usize,\n\n        /// Output format (text, json, toon)\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"text\")]\n        format: String,\n\n        /// Output JSON (alias for --format json)\n        #[arg(long)]\n        json: bool,\n    },\n\n    /// Claim a bead (atomic)\n    Claim {\n        /// Assignee (worker ID)\n        #[arg(long)]\n        assignee: String,\n\n        /// Model\n        #[arg(long)]\n        model: Option<String>,\n\n        /// Harness\n        #[arg(long)]\n        harness: Option<String>,\n\n        /// Harness version\n        #[arg(long)]\n        harness_version: Option<String>,\n\n        /// Claim from any workspace (searches all .beads/ directories)\n        #[arg(long)]\n        any: bool,\n\n        /// Fallback mode: try current workspace first, fall back to any if no beads available\n        #[arg(long)]\n        fallback: bool,\n\n        /// Workspace paths to search (only used with --any)\n        #[arg(long)]\n        workspace_paths: Vec<PathBuf>,\n\n        /// Dry run (show what would be claimed without claiming)\n        #[arg(long)]\n        dry_run: bool,\n\n        /// Output format (text, json, toon)\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"text\")]\n        format: String,\n\n        /// Output JSON (alias for --format json)\n        #[arg(long)]\n        json: bool,\n    },\n\n    /// Initialize a new workspace\n    Init {\n        /// Issue prefix\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"bf\")]\n        prefix: String,\n    },\n\n    /// Sync (flush to JSONL or import from JSONL)\n    Sync {\n        /// Flush only (SQLite -> JSONL)\n        #[arg(long)]\n        flush_only: bool,\n\n        /// Import only (JSONL -> SQLite)\n        #[arg(long)]\n        import_only: bool,\n    },\n\n    /// Doctor - check and repair\n    Doctor {\n        /// Repair database\n        #[arg(long)]\n        repair: bool,\n    },\n\n    /// Count beads\n    Count {\n        /// Filter by status\n        #[arg(long)]\n        status: Option<String>,\n    },\n\n    /// Batch operations (atomic)\n    Batch {\n        /// JSON file containing operations\n        #[arg(long)]\n        file: Option<PathBuf>,\n\n        /// JSON string containing operations\n        #[arg(long)]\n        json: Option<String>,\n\n        /// Read from stdin\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"false\")]\n        stdin: bool,\n    },\n\n    /// Mitosis: split a bead into children atomically\n    Mitosis {\n        /// Parent bead ID to split\n        id: String,\n\n        /// Child bead definitions (JSON array of {title, type, priority})\n        #[arg(long)]\n        children: String,\n\n        /// Close reason for parent bead\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"Split into children\")]\n        reason: String,\n\n        /// Output format (text, json, toon)\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"text\")]\n        format: String,\n    },\n\n    /// Manage dependencies\n    #[command(subcommand)]\n    Dep(DepCommands),\n\n    /// Manage labels\n    #[command(subcommand)]\n    Label(LabelCommands),\n\n    /// List labels for a specific issue (direct SELECT, efficient)\n    Labels {\n        /// Bead ID\n        id: String,\n\n        /// Output format (text, json)\n        #[arg(short, long, default_value = \"text\")]\n        format: String,\n    },\n\n    /// Manage comments\n    #[command(subcommand)]\n    Comments(CommentsCommands),\n\n    /// Search beads\n    Search {\n        /// Search query\n        query: Option<String>,\n\n        /// Filter by status\n        #[arg(short, long)]\n        status: Vec<String>,\n\n        /// Filter by type\n        #[arg(short, long)]\n        type_: Vec<String>,\n\n        /// Filter by assignee\n        #[arg(long)]\n        assignee: Option<String>,\n\n        /// Filter by label\n        #[arg(short, long)]\n        label: Vec<String>,\n\n        /// Filter by minimum priority\n        #[arg(long, value_name = \"MIN\")]\n        priority_min: Option<i32>,\n\n        /// Filter by maximum priority\n        #[arg(long, value_name = \"MAX\")]\n        priority_max: Option<i32>,\n\n        /// Limit results\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"50\")]\n        limit: usize,\n\n        /// Output format (text, json, toon)\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"text\")]\n        format: String,\n    },\n\n    /// Show statistics\n    Stats {\n        /// Show breakdown by type\n        #[arg(long)]\n        by_type: bool,\n\n        /// Show breakdown by priority\n        #[arg(long)]\n        by_priority: bool,\n\n        /// Show breakdown by assignee\n        #[arg(long)]\n        by_assignee: bool,\n\n        /// Show breakdown by label\n        #[arg(long)]\n        by_label: bool,\n\n        /// Output format (text, json, toon)\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"text\")]\n        format: String,\n    },\n\n    /// Emit JSON Schema\n    Schema {\n        /// Schema target\n        #[arg(default_value = \"all\")]\n        target: String,\n\n        /// Output format (text, json)\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"json\")]\n        format: String,\n    },\n\n    /// Configuration management\n    #[command(subcommand)]\n    Config(ConfigCommands),\n\n    /// Show velocity stats (bead-forge specific)\n    Velocity {\n        /// Model\n        #[arg(long)]\n        model: Option<String>,\n\n        /// Harness\n        #[arg(long)]\n        harness: Option<String>,\n\n        /// Output format (text, json, toon)\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"text\")]\n        format: String,\n    },\n\n    /// Manage annotations\n    #[command(subcommand)]\n    Annotate(AnnotateCommands),\n\n    /// Show event log for a bead\n    Log {\n        /// Bead ID (omit to show all events)\n        id: Option<String>,\n\n        /// Limit number of entries\n        #[arg(long)]\n        limit: Option<usize>,\n\n        /// Show events since this date (RFC3339 format, e.g., 2026-05-01T00:00:00Z)\n        #[arg(long)]\n        since: Option<String>,\n\n        /// Filter by actor (worker name)\n        #[arg(long)]\n        actor: Option<String>,\n\n        /// Show only status change events\n        #[arg(long)]\n        status_changes: bool,\n\n        /// Show field-level diff between old and new values\n        #[arg(long)]\n        diff: bool,\n\n        /// Output format (text, json, toon)\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"text\")]\n        format: String,\n\n        /// Output JSON (alias for --format json)\n        #[arg(long)]\n        json: bool,\n    },\n\n    /// Show critical path (longest chain of blocking dependencies)\n    CriticalPath {\n        /// Root bead ID\n        id: String,\n\n        /// Maximum depth\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"20\")]\n        max_depth: usize,\n\n        /// Output format (text, json, toon)\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"text\")]\n        format: String,\n    },\n\n    /// Rotate (archive) closed beads older than threshold\n    Rotate {\n        /// Days threshold (archive beads closed this many days ago)\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"30\")]\n        days: u64,\n\n        /// Dry run (show what would be rotated)\n        #[arg(long)]\n        dry_run: bool,\n    },\n\n    /// Migrate workspace from br to bf\n    Migrate {\n        /// Workspace path to migrate (defaults to current directory)\n        #[arg(short, long)]\n        workspace: Option<PathBuf>,\n\n        /// Reimport from JSONL (for corrupted/missing databases)\n        #[arg(long)]\n        from_jsonl: bool,\n\n        /// Seed velocity stats from reconstructed events\n        #[arg(long)]\n        seed_velocity: bool,\n\n        /// Dry run (show what would be done without making changes)\n        #[arg(long)]\n        dry_run: bool,\n\n        /// Skip verification steps\n        #[arg(long)]\n        skip_verify: bool,\n    },\n}\n\n#[derive(Subcommand)]\npub enum DepCommands {\n    /// Add a dependency\n    Add {\n        /// Issue ID (the one that will depend on something)\n        issue: String,\n\n        /// Target issue ID (the one being depended on)\n        depends_on: String,\n\n        /// Dependency type\n        #[arg(short, long, default_value = \"blocks\")]\n        type_: String,\n    },\n\n    /// Remove a dependency\n    Remove {\n        /// Issue ID\n        issue: String,\n\n        /// Target issue ID to remove dependency to\n        depends_on: String,\n    },\n\n    /// List dependencies of an issue\n    List {\n        /// Issue ID\n        id: String,\n    },\n\n    /// Show dependency tree rooted at issue\n    Tree {\n        /// Issue ID (root of tree)\n        id: String,\n\n        /// Tree direction (down, up, both)\n        #[arg(short, long, default_value = \"down\")]\n        direction: String,\n\n        /// Maximum depth\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"10\")]\n        max_depth: usize,\n    },\n}\n\n#[derive(Subcommand)]\npub enum LabelCommands {\n    /// Add label(s) to an issue\n    Add {\n        /// Label(s) to add (multiple labels supported)\n        #[arg(short, long, required = true, num_args = 1..)]\n        label: Vec<String>,\n\n        /// Issue ID\n        id: String,\n    },\n\n    /// Remove label(s) from an issue\n    Remove {\n        /// Label(s) to remove (multiple labels supported)\n        #[arg(short, long, required = true, num_args = 1..)]\n        label: Vec<String>,\n\n        /// Issue ID\n        id: String,\n    },\n\n    /// List labels for an issue or all unique labels\n    List {\n        /// Issue ID (optional - if omitted, lists all unique labels)\n        id: Option<String>,\n    },\n}\n\n#[derive(Subcommand)]\npub enum CommentsCommands {\n    /// Add a comment\n    Add {\n        /// Issue ID\n        id: String,\n\n        /// Comment text\n        #[arg(required = true, num_args = 1..)]\n        text: Vec<String>,\n    },\n\n    /// List comments for an issue\n    List {\n        /// Issue ID\n        id: String,\n    },\n}\n\n#[derive(Subcommand)]\npub enum ConfigCommands {\n    /// List all config values\n    List,\n\n    /// Get a specific config value\n    Get {\n        /// Config key\n        key: String,\n    },\n\n    /// Show config file path\n    Path,\n}\n\n#[derive(Subcommand)]\npub enum AnnotateCommands {\n    /// Set an annotation\n    Set {\n        /// Issue ID\n        id: String,\n\n        /// Annotation key\n        key: String,\n\n        /// Annotation value\n        value: String,\n    },\n\n    /// Get an annotation\n    Get {\n        /// Issue ID\n        id: String,\n\n        /// Annotation key\n        key: String,\n    },\n\n    /// Remove an annotation\n    Remove {\n        /// Issue ID\n        id: String,\n\n        /// Annotation key\n        key: String,\n    },\n\n    /// List all annotations for an issue\n    List {\n        /// Issue ID\n        id: String,\n    },\n\n    /// Clear all annotations for an issue\n    Clear {\n        /// Issue ID\n        id: String,\n    },\n}\n\npub fn run_cli() -> Result<Cli> {\n    Ok(Cli::try_parse()?)\n}\n\npub fn run(cli: Cli) -> Result<()> {\n    let workspace = cli.workspace.unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from(\".\"));\n\n    // For init command, we allow the .beads directory to not exist yet\n    match &cli.command {\n        Commands::Init { .. } => {\n            let beads_dir = workspace.join(\".beads\");\n            return match cli.command {\n                Commands::Init { prefix } => cmd_init(&beads_dir, &prefix),\n                _ => unreachable!(),\n            };\n        }\n        _ => {}\n    }\n\n    let beads_dir = find_beads_dir(&workspace)\n        .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!(\"No .beads directory found in {:?}\", workspace))?;\n\n    match cli.command {\n        Commands::Init { prefix } => cmd_init(&beads_dir, &prefix),\n        Commands::Create { title, type_, priority, description, assignee, label } => {\n            cmd_create(&beads_dir, title, type_, priority, description, assignee, label)\n        }\n        Commands::List { status, type_, assignee, priority, limit, all, format, json } => {\n            let format = if json { \"json\".to_string() } else { format };\n            cmd_list(&beads_dir, status, type_, assignee, priority, limit, all, &format)\n        }\n        Commands::Show { id, format, json } => {\n            let format = if json { \"json\".to_string() } else { format };\n            cmd_show(&beads_dir, &id, &format)\n        }\n        Commands::Update { id, title, status, priority, assignee } => {\n            cmd_update(&beads_dir, &id, title, status, priority, assignee)\n        }\n        Commands::Close { id, reason } => cmd_close(&beads_dir, &id, &reason),\n        Commands::Reopen { id } => cmd_reopen(&beads_dir, &id),\n        Commands::Delete { id } => cmd_delete(&beads_dir, &id),\n        Commands::Ready { limit, format, json } => {\n            let format = if json { \"json\".to_string() } else { format };\n            cmd_ready(&beads_dir, limit, &format)\n        }\n        Commands::Claim { assignee, model, harness, harness_version, any, fallback, workspace_paths, dry_run, format, json } => {\n            let format = if json { \"json\".to_string() } else { format };\n            cmd_claim(&beads_dir, &assignee, model, harness, harness_version, any, fallback, &workspace_paths, dry_run, &format)\n        }\n        Commands::Sync { flush_only, import_only } => cmd_sync(&beads_dir, flush_only, import_only),\n        Commands::Doctor { repair } => cmd_doctor(&beads_dir, repair),\n        Commands::Count { status } => cmd_count(&beads_dir, status),\n        Commands::Batch { file, json, stdin } => cmd_batch(&beads_dir, file, json, stdin),\n        Commands::Mitosis { id, children, reason, format } => cmd_mitosis(&beads_dir, &id, &children, &reason, &format),\n        Commands::Dep(dep) => cmd_dep(&beads_dir, dep),\n        Commands::Label(label) => cmd_label(&beads_dir, label),\n        Commands::Comments(comments) => cmd_comments(&beads_dir, comments),\n        Commands::Search { query, status, type_, assignee, label, priority_min, priority_max, limit, format } => {\n            cmd_search(&beads_dir, query, status, type_, assignee, label, priority_min, priority_max, limit, &format)\n        }\n        Commands::Stats { by_type, by_priority, by_assignee, by_label, format } => {\n            cmd_stats(&beads_dir, by_type, by_priority, by_assignee, by_label, &format)\n        }\n        Commands::Schema { target, format } => cmd_schema(&target, &format),\n        Commands::Config(config) => cmd_config(&beads_dir, config),\n        Commands::Velocity { model, harness, format } => cmd_velocity(&beads_dir, model, harness, &format),\n        Commands::Labels { id, format } => cmd_labels(&beads_dir, &id, &format),\n        Commands::Annotate(annotate) => cmd_annotate(&beads_dir, annotate),\n        Commands::Log { id, limit, since, actor, status_changes, diff, format, json } => {\n            let format = if json { \"json\".to_string() } else { format };\n            cmd_log(&beads_dir, id, limit, since, actor, status_changes, diff, &format)\n        }\n        Commands::CriticalPath { id, max_depth, format } => cmd_critical_path(&beads_dir, &id, max_depth, &format),\n        Commands::Rotate { days, dry_run } => cmd_rotate(&beads_dir, days, dry_run),\n        Commands::Migrate { workspace, from_jsonl, seed_velocity, dry_run, skip_verify } => {\n            cmd_migrate(&beads_dir, workspace, from_jsonl, seed_velocity, dry_run, skip_verify)\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nfn cmd_init(beads_dir: &PathBuf, prefix: &str) -> Result<()> {\n    std::fs::create_dir_all(beads_dir)?;\n\n    let config_path = beads_dir.join(\"config.yaml\");\n    if !config_path.exists() {\n        let config = format!(\n            r#\"issue_prefixes: [{}]\ndefault_priority: 2\ndefault_type: task\nclaim_ttl_minutes: 30\n\"#,\n            prefix\n        );\n        std::fs::write(&config_path, config)?;\n    }\n\n    let metadata_path = beads_dir.join(\"metadata.json\");\n    if !metadata_path.exists() {\n        let metadata = r#\"{\"database\": \"beads.db\", \"jsonl_export\": \"issues.jsonl\"}\"#;\n        std::fs::write(&metadata_path, metadata)?;\n    }\n\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(\"beads.db\");\n    let _storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n    let gitignore_path = beads_dir.join(\".gitignore\");\n    if !gitignore_path.exists() {\n        std::fs::write(&gitignore_path, \"beads.db\\nbeads.db-shm\\nbeads.db-wal\\n\")?;\n    }\n\n    println!(\"Initialized bead-forge workspace in {:?}\", beads_dir);\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_create(\n    beads_dir: &PathBuf,\n    title: String,\n    type_: String,\n    priority: i32,\n    description: Option<String>,\n    assignee: Option<String>,\n    labels: Vec<String>,\n) -> Result<()> {\n    let config = load_config(beads_dir)?;\n    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n    let count = storage.count_issues()?;\n    let prefix = get_default_prefix(&config);\n    let id = crate::id::generate_id(prefix, count);\n\n    let mut issue = Issue::new(id.clone(), title, \".\".to_string());\n    issue.issue_type = IssueType::from_str(type_.as_str()).map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?;\n    issue.priority = Priority(priority);\n    issue.description = description;\n    issue.assignee = assignee;\n    issue.labels = labels;\n\n    storage.create_issue(&issue)?;\n\n    println!(\"{}\", id);\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_list(\n    beads_dir: &PathBuf,\n    status: Option<String>,\n    type_: Option<String>,\n    assignee: Option<String>,\n    priority: Option<i32>,\n    limit: Option<usize>,\n    all: bool,\n    format: &str,\n) -> Result<()> {\n    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n    let mut issues = if all {\n        // Include archived beads\n        list_all_with_archives(beads_dir)?\n    } else {\n        // Only from database\n        let mut filter = IssueFilter::default();\n        if let Some(ref s) = status {\n            filter.status = Some(Status::from_str(s.as_str()).map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?);\n        }\n        if let Some(ref t) = type_ {\n            filter.issue_type = Some(IssueType::from_str(t.as_str()).map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?);\n        }\n        filter.assignee = assignee.clone();\n        filter.priority = priority;\n        // --limit 0 means unlimited\n        filter.limit = limit.and_then(|l| if l == 0 { None } else { Some(l) });\n        storage.list_issues(&filter)?\n    };\n\n    // Apply additional filters for --all mode (since we're not using DB filters)\n    if all {\n        if let Some(ref s) = status {\n            let status_filter = Status::from_str(s.as_str()).map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?;\n            issues.retain(|i| i.status == status_filter);\n        }\n        if let Some(ref t) = type_ {\n            let type_filter = IssueType::from_str(t.as_str()).map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?;\n            issues.retain(|i| i.issue_type == type_filter);\n        }\n        if let Some(ref assignee_val) = assignee {\n            issues.retain(|i| i.assignee.as_deref() == Some(assignee_val));\n        }\n        if let Some(p) = priority {\n            issues.retain(|i| i.priority.0 == p);\n        }\n        // Apply limit\n        if let Some(l) = limit {\n            if l != 0 {\n                issues.truncate(l);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    let output_format = OutputFormat::from_str(format).unwrap_or(OutputFormat::Text);\n    let formatter = get_formatter(output_format);\n    print!(\"{}\", formatter.format_issues(&issues));\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_show(beads_dir: &PathBuf, id: &str, format: &str) -> Result<()> {\n    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n    let issue = match storage.get_issue(id)? {\n        Some(i) => i,\n        None => {\n            // Search archives\n            find_bead_in_archives(beads_dir, id)?\n                .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!(\"Bead not found: {}\", id))?\n        }\n    };\n\n    match format {\n        \"json\" => {\n            // Strip dependencies/comments before serializing: NEEDLE's BrDependency\n            // format ({id, title, status, priority, dependency_type}) differs from\n            // bead-forge's Dependency format ({issue_id, depends_on_id, type, ...}).\n            // NEEDLE has #[serde(default)] on the deps field so empty is fine.\n            let mut out = issue;\n            out.dependencies = vec![];\n            out.comments = vec![];\n            // Wrap in array so NEEDLE's parse_single_bead (Vec<Bead> → first) works.\n            println!(\"{}\", serde_json::to_string(&vec![out])?);\n        }\n        \"toon\" => {\n            println!(\"ID: {}\", issue.id);\n            println!(\"Title: {}\", issue.title);\n            println!(\"Status: {}\", issue.status);\n            println!(\"Priority: {}\", issue.priority);\n            println!(\"Type: {}\", issue.issue_type);\n            if let Some(desc) = &issue.description {\n                println!(\"Description: {}\", desc);\n            }\n            if let Some(assignee) = &issue.assignee {\n                println!(\"Assignee: {}\", assignee);\n            }\n            if !issue.labels.is_empty() {\n                println!(\"Labels: {}\", issue.labels.join(\", \"));\n            }\n        }\n        _ => {\n            println!(\"ID: {}\", issue.id);\n            println!(\"Title: {}\", issue.title);\n            println!(\"Status: {}\", issue.status);\n            println!(\"Priority: {}\", issue.priority);\n            println!(\"Type: {}\", issue.issue_type);\n            if let Some(desc) = &issue.description {\n                println!(\"Description: {}\", desc);\n            }\n            if let Some(assignee) = &issue.assignee {\n                println!(\"Assignee: {}\", assignee);\n            }\n            if !issue.labels.is_empty() {\n                println!(\"Labels: {}\", issue.labels.join(\", \"));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_update(\n    beads_dir: &PathBuf,\n    id: &str,\n    title: Option<String>,\n    status: Option<String>,\n    priority: Option<i32>,\n    assignee: Option<String>,\n) -> Result<()> {\n    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n    let changes = IssueChanges {\n        title,\n        status: status.map(|s| Status::from_str(&s).ok()).flatten(),\n        priority,\n        assignee,\n        ..Default::default()\n    };\n\n    storage.update_issue(id, &changes)?;\n    println!(\"Updated bead {}\", id);\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_close(beads_dir: &PathBuf, id: &str, reason: &str) -> Result<()> {\n    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n    storage.close_issue(id, reason, \"cli\")?;\n    println!(\"Closed bead {}\", id);\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_reopen(beads_dir: &PathBuf, id: &str) -> Result<()> {\n    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n    let changes = IssueChanges {\n        status: Some(Status::Open),\n        ..Default::default()\n    };\n\n    storage.update_issue(id, &changes)?;\n    println!(\"Reopened bead {}\", id);\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_delete(beads_dir: &PathBuf, id: &str) -> Result<()> {\n    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n    storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n        tx.execute(\"DELETE FROM issues WHERE id = ?\", [&id])?;\n        Ok(())\n    })?;\n\n    println!(\"Deleted bead {}\", id);\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_ready(beads_dir: &PathBuf, limit: usize, format: &str) -> Result<()> {\n    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n    let candidates = storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n        get_ready_candidates(tx, limit)\n    })?;\n\n    match format {\n        \"json\" => {\n            for candidate in candidates {\n                println!(\"{}\", serde_json::to_string(&candidate)?);\n            }\n        }\n        \"toon\" => {\n            for candidate in candidates {\n                println!(\"{}\", crate::format::toon::format_ready_bead(\n                    &candidate.id,\n                    &candidate.title,\n                    candidate.priority,\n                    candidate.downstream_impact,\n                    candidate.critical_float,\n                ));\n            }\n        }\n        _ => {\n            for candidate in candidates {\n                println!(\"[{}] {} (priority={}, impact={}, float={})\",\n                    candidate.id, candidate.title, candidate.priority,\n                    candidate.downstream_impact, candidate.critical_float);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_claim(\n    beads_dir: &PathBuf,\n    assignee: &str,\n    model: Option<String>,\n    harness: Option<String>,\n    harness_version: Option<String>,\n    any: bool,\n    fallback: bool,\n    workspace_paths: &[PathBuf],\n    dry_run: bool,\n    format: &str,\n) -> Result<()> {\n    let config = load_config(beads_dir)?;\n    let claim_ttl = config.claim_ttl_minutes;\n\n    // Build worker metadata\n    let worker_metadata = WorkerMetadata {\n        worker_id: assignee.to_string(),\n        model: model.clone(),\n        harness: harness.clone(),\n        harness_version: harness_version.clone(),\n    };\n\n    if dry_run {\n        // Dry run mode - show what would be claimed\n        let candidates: Vec<(PathBuf, crate::claim::ScoredBead)> = if any || fallback {\n            // Multi-workspace dry run\n            let paths = if workspace_paths.is_empty() {\n                // Auto-discover workspaces from current directory\n                find_workspaces(&std::env::current_dir()?)?\n            } else {\n                workspace_paths.to_vec()\n            };\n\n            let mut all_candidates = Vec::new();\n            for path in &paths {\n                let local_beads_dir = path.join(\".beads\");\n                if local_beads_dir.exists() {\n                    let local_metadata = match load_metadata(&local_beads_dir) {\n                        Ok(m) => m,\n                        Err(_) => continue,\n                    };\n                    let local_db_path = local_beads_dir.join(&local_metadata.database);\n                    if let Ok(local_storage) = Storage::open(&local_db_path) {\n                        if let Ok(local_candidates) = local_storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n                            get_ready_candidates(tx, 1)\n                        }) {\n                            for c in local_candidates {\n                                all_candidates.push((path.clone(), c));\n                            }\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n\n            // Sort by score and take top 1\n            all_candidates.sort_by(|a, b| {\n                let score_a = (b.1.downstream_impact, b.1.priority, b.1.created_at.clone());\n                let score_b = (a.1.downstream_impact, a.1.priority, a.1.created_at.clone());\n                score_a.cmp(&score_b)\n            });\n            all_candidates.into_iter().take(1).collect()\n        } else {\n            // Single workspace dry run\n            let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n            let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n            let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n            let candidates = storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n                get_ready_candidates(tx, 1)\n            })?;\n            candidates.into_iter().map(|c| (beads_dir.parent().unwrap_or(beads_dir).to_path_buf(), c)).collect()\n        };\n\n        if let Some((path, candidate)) = candidates.first() {\n            match format {\n                \"json\" => {\n                    let output = serde_json::json!({\n                        \"bead_id\": candidate.id,\n                        \"title\": candidate.title,\n                        \"priority\": candidate.priority,\n                        \"downstream_impact\": candidate.downstream_impact,\n                        \"assignee\": assignee,\n                        \"workspace\": path.display().to_string(),\n                        \"dry_run\": true\n                    });\n                    println!(\"{}\", output);\n                }\n                _ => {\n                    println!(\"{} (priority={}, impact={}, workspace={})\",\n                        candidate.id, candidate.priority, candidate.downstream_impact, path.display());\n                }\n            }\n        } else if format == \"json\" {\n            println!(\"{{}}\");\n        } else {\n            println!(\"No beads available to claim\");\n        }\n    } else if any {\n        // Claim from any workspace\n        let paths = if workspace_paths.is_empty() {\n            // Auto-discover workspaces from current directory\n            find_workspaces(&std::env::current_dir()?)?\n        } else {\n            workspace_paths.to_vec()\n        };\n\n        let result = claim_any(&paths, assignee, claim_ttl, Some(&worker_metadata))?;\n\n        match result {\n            Some(ClaimResult { bead_id, reclaimed, workspace_path }) => {\n                match format {\n                    \"json\" => {\n                        let output = serde_json::json!({\n                            \"bead_id\": bead_id,\n                            \"reclaimed\": reclaimed,\n                            \"assignee\": assignee,\n                            \"workspace\": workspace_path.map(|p| p.display().to_string())\n                        });\n                        println!(\"{}\", output);\n                    }\n                    _ => {\n                        if let Some(path) = workspace_path {\n                            println!(\"{} (workspace: {})\", bead_id, path.display());\n                        } else {\n                            println!(\"{}\", bead_id);\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            None => {\n                if format == \"json\" {\n                    println!(\"{{}}\");\n                } else {\n                    println!(\"No beads available to claim\");\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    } else if fallback {\n        // Fallback mode: try current workspace first, then any\n        let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n        let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n        let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n        let result = storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n            claim(tx, assignee, claim_ttl, Utc::now(), Some(&worker_metadata))\n        })?;\n\n        match result {\n            Some(ClaimResult { bead_id, reclaimed, .. }) => {\n                match format {\n                    \"json\" => {\n                        let output = serde_json::json!({\n                            \"bead_id\": bead_id,\n                            \"reclaimed\": reclaimed,\n                            \"assignee\": assignee\n                        });\n                        println!(\"{}\", output);\n                    }\n                    _ => {\n                        println!(\"{}\", bead_id);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            None => {\n                // Fallback to any workspace\n                let paths = if workspace_paths.is_empty() {\n                    find_workspaces(&std::env::current_dir()?)?\n                } else {\n                    workspace_paths.to_vec()\n                };\n\n                let result = claim_any(&paths, assignee, claim_ttl, Some(&worker_metadata))?;\n\n                match result {\n                    Some(ClaimResult { bead_id, reclaimed, workspace_path }) => {\n                        match format {\n                            \"json\" => {\n                                let output = serde_json::json!({\n                                    \"bead_id\": bead_id,\n                                    \"reclaimed\": reclaimed,\n                                    \"assignee\": assignee,\n                                    \"workspace\": workspace_path.map(|p| p.display().to_string())\n                                });\n                                println!(\"{}\", output);\n                            }\n                            _ => {\n                                if let Some(path) = workspace_path {\n                                    println!(\"{} (workspace: {})\", bead_id, path.display());\n                                } else {\n                                    println!(\"{}\", bead_id);\n                                }\n                            }\n                        }\n                    }\n                    None => {\n                        if format == \"json\" {\n                            println!(\"{{}}\");\n                        } else {\n                            println!(\"No beads available to claim\");\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    } else {\n        // Normal single-workspace claim\n        let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n        let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n        let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n        let result = storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n            claim(tx, assignee, claim_ttl, Utc::now(), Some(&worker_metadata))\n        })?;\n\n        match result {\n            Some(ClaimResult { bead_id, reclaimed, .. }) => {\n                match format {\n                    \"json\" => {\n                        let output = serde_json::json!({\n                            \"bead_id\": bead_id,\n                            \"reclaimed\": reclaimed,\n                            \"assignee\": assignee\n                        });\n                        println!(\"{}\", output);\n                    }\n                    _ => {\n                        println!(\"{}\", bead_id);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            None => {\n                if format == \"json\" {\n                    println!(\"{{}}\");\n                } else {\n                    println!(\"No beads available to claim\");\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_sync(beads_dir: &PathBuf, flush_only: bool, import_only: bool) -> Result<()> {\n    let workspace_dir = beads_dir.parent().unwrap_or(beads_dir);\n\n    if import_only {\n        let result = crate::sync::import(workspace_dir)?;\n        println!(\"Imported {} beads\", result.imported);\n        if result.updated > 0 {\n            println!(\"Updated {} beads\", result.updated);\n        }\n        if result.skipped > 0 {\n            println!(\"Skipped {} unchanged beads\", result.skipped);\n        }\n    } else if flush_only {\n        let count = crate::sync::flush(workspace_dir)?;\n        println!(\"Flushed {} beads to JSONL\", count);\n    } else {\n        let result = crate::sync::sync(workspace_dir)?;\n        println!(\"Synced {} beads from JSONL and flushed {} to JSONL\", result.imported + result.updated, result.exported);\n        if result.updated > 0 {\n            println!(\"Updated {} beads\", result.updated);\n        }\n        if result.skipped > 0 {\n            println!(\"Skipped {} unchanged beads\", result.skipped);\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_doctor(beads_dir: &PathBuf, repair: bool) -> Result<()> {\n    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n\n    if repair {\n        std::fs::remove_file(&db_path)?;\n        let jsonl_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.jsonl_export);\n        let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n        let result = storage.sync_from_jsonl(&jsonl_path)?;\n        println!(\"Repaired database: imported {} beads from JSONL\", result.imported);\n    } else {\n        let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n        let count = storage.count_issues()?;\n        println!(\"Database is healthy: {} beads\", count);\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_count(beads_dir: &PathBuf, status: Option<String>) -> Result<()> {\n    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n    let count = if let Some(s) = status {\n        let filter = IssueFilter {\n            status: Some(Status::from_str(&s).map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?),\n            ..Default::default()\n        };\n        storage.list_issues(&filter)?.len()\n    } else {\n        storage.count_issues()?\n    };\n\n    println!(\"{}\", count);\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_batch(beads_dir: &PathBuf, file: Option<PathBuf>, json: Option<String>, stdin: bool) -> Result<()> {\n    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n    let ops: Vec<BatchOp> = if let Some(json_str) = json {\n        serde_json::from_str(&json_str)?\n    } else if let Some(file_path) = file {\n        let content = std::fs::read_to_string(&file_path)?;\n        serde_json::from_str(&content)?\n    } else if stdin {\n        parse_stdin()?\n    } else {\n        return Err(anyhow!(\"Must provide --file, --json, or --stdin\"));\n    };\n\n    let results = execute_batch(&storage, ops, beads_dir)?;\n\n    // Print results\n    for result in results {\n        if result.status == \"ok\" {\n            if let Some(id) = result.id {\n                println!(\"[op {}] ok: {}\", result.op, id);\n            } else {\n                println!(\"[op {}] ok\", result.op);\n            }\n        } else {\n            eprintln!(\"[op {}] error: {}\", result.op, result.error.unwrap_or_default());\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_mitosis(beads_dir: &PathBuf, id: &str, children: &str, reason: &str, format: &str) -> Result<()> {\n    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n    // Parse children as JSON array of {title, type, priority} or {title, type, priority, description, assignee, labels}\n    let children_defs: Vec<MitosisChild> = serde_json::from_str(children)?;\n\n    // Build the batch operations\n    let ops = mitosis_ex(id, children_defs, Some(reason.to_string()))?;\n\n    // Execute atomically\n    let results = execute_batch(&storage, ops, beads_dir)?;\n\n    match format {\n        \"json\" => {\n            println!(\"{}\", serde_json::to_string_pretty(&results)?);\n        }\n        _ => {\n            // Print child IDs that were created\n            for println!(\"Parent bead {} closed with {} children\", id, results.len() - 2); // -2 for close + last dep\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_dep(beads_dir: &PathBuf, dep: DepCommands) -> Result<()> {\n    match dep {\n        DepCommands::Add { issue, depends_on, type_ } => {\n            let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n            let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n            let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n            let dep_type = crate::model::DependencyType::from_str(&type_)\n                .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?;\n            storage.add_dependency(&issue, &depends_on, &dep_type, \"cli\")?;\n            println!(\"Added dependency: {} depends on {} ({})\", issue, depends_on, type_);\n        }\n        DepCommands::Remove { issue, depends_on } => {\n            let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n            let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n            let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n            storage.remove_dependency(&issue, &depends_on)?;\n            println!(\"Removed dependency: {} -> {}\", issue, depends_on);\n        }\n        DepCommands::List { id } => {\n            let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n            let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n            let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n            let deps = storage.get_dependencies(&id)?;\n            if deps.is_empty() {\n                println!(\"No dependencies found for {}\", id);\n            } else {\n                for dep in deps {\n                    println!(\"  {} depends on {} ({})\", dep.issue_id, dep.depends_on_id, dep.dep_type);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        DepCommands::Tree { id, direction: _, max_depth: _ } => {\n            println!(\"Dependency tree for {}\", id);\n            println!(\"(tree view not yet implemented)\");\n        }\n    }\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_label(beads_dir: &PathBuf, label: LabelCommands) -> Result<()> {\n    match label {\n        LabelCommands::Add { id, label } => {\n            let metadata = }\n    }\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_velocity(\n    beads_dir: &PathBuf,\n    model: Option<String>,\n    harness: Option<String>,\n    format: &str,\n) -> Result<()> {\n    let metadata = println!(\"{}\", serde_json::to_string_pretty(&stats)?);\n        }\n        \"toon\" => {\n            for stat in stats {\n                println!(\"Model: {}\", stat.model);\n                println!(\"Harness: {}\", stat.harness);\n                println!(\"Type: {}\", stat.issue_type);\n                println!(\"Samples: {}\", stat.sample_count);\n                if let Some(p50) = stat.p50_seconds {\n                    println!(\"P50: {}s\", p50);\n                }\n                if let Some(p90) = stat.p90_seconds {\n                    println!(\"P90: {}s\", p90);\n                }\n                if let Some(avg) = stat.avg_seconds {\n                    println!(\"Avg: {:.1}s\", avg);\n                }\n                println!();\n            }\n        }\n        _ => {\n            if stats.is_empty() {\n                println!(\"No velocity statistics available yet.\");\n                println!(\"Velocity data accumulates as beads are claimed and closed.\");\n            } else {\n                println!(\"Velocity Statistics:\");\n                println!();\n                println!(\"{:<20} {:<15} {:<10} {:<8} {:<8} {:<8} {:<8}\", \"Model\", \"Harness\", \"Type\", \"Samples\", \"P50(s)\", \"P90(s)\", \"Avg(s)\");\n                println!(\"{}\", \"-\".repeat(85));\n                for stat in stats {\n                    println!(\"{:<20} {:<15} {:<10} {:<8} {:<8} {:<8} {:<8.1}\",\n                        stat.model,\n                        stat.harness,\n                        stat.issue_type,\n                        stat.sample_count,\n                        stat.p50_seconds.map(|s| s.to_string()).unwrap_or_else(|| \"-\".to_string()),\n                        stat.p90_seconds.map(|s| diff: bool,\n    format: &str,\n) -> Result<()> {\n    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n limit {\n        filter = filter.with_limit(limit_val);\n    }\n\n    if let Some(actor_val) = actor {\n        filter = filter.with_actor(actor_val);\n    }\n\n    if status_changes {\n        filter = filter.status_changes_only();\n    }\n\n    if diff {\n        filter = filter.with_diff();\n    }\n\n    // Parse since date if provided\n    if let Some(since_str) = since {\n        match chrono::DateTime::parse_from_rfc3339(&since_str) {\n            Ok(dt) => {\n                filter = filter.with_since(dt.with_timezone(&chrono::Utc));\n            }\n            Err(_) => {\n                return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(\"Invalid --since date format. Use RFC3339 format, e.g., 2026-05-01T00:00:00Z\"));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Query events\n    let events = crate::log::query_events(&storage, &filter)?;\n\n    match format {\n        \"json\" => {\n            println!(\"{}\", crate::log::format_events_json(&events)?);\n        }\n        \"toon\" => {\n            for event in &events {\n                println!(\"{}\", crate::log::format_event_toon(event));\n            }\n        }\n        _ => {\n            if events.is_empty() {\n                println!(\"No events found\");\n            } else {\n                for event in &events {\n                    println!(\"{}\", crate::log::format_event_text(event, diff));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_critical_path(beads_dir: &PathBuf, id: &str, _max_depth: usize, format: &str) -> Result<()> {\n    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = println!(\"Longest chain: {}\", result.longest_chain.join(\" → \"));\n                println!(\"Minimum remaining time: {} bead-completions on critical path\", result.min_remaining);\n            }\n    if let Some(ref archive_path) = result.archive_path {\n            println!(\"Created archive: {}\", archive_path.display());\n        }\n        println!(\"{} beads remain in active file\", result.remaining);\n\n        if !result.deleted_archives.is_empty() {\n            println!(\"Deleted {} old archive(s):\", result.deleted_archives.len());\n            for path in Since the worker closes the bead when done (writing a new entry), active workers never get reclaimed.\n444\t\n445\t**Manual stale scan**: For workspaces with no active claiming (no fleet running), `bf doctor --reclaim-stale` performs the same scan without claiming a bead. Since the worker closes the bead when done (writing a new entry), active workers never get reclaimed.\n\n**Manual stale scan**: For workspaces with no active claiming (no fleet running), `bf doctor --reclaim-stale` performs the same scan without claiming a bead. This was removed because:\n\n1. **SQLite is sufficient**: `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` provides serialized write access. Claim latency is ~0.5-2ms — 4-16× faster than the previous flock+streaming-rewrite approach.\n2. **Simpler operations**: No flock infrastructure, no streaming rewrite, no temp files during normal operation. All mutation logic is SQL.\n3. **Crash recovery is automatic**: SQLite rolls back incomplete transactions on next open. No partial state, no temp file cleanup.\n4. **Real-world scale validates it**: A 2,000-bead workspace takes ~2ms per claim. A 20-worker fleet needs ~1-2 claims/sec. SQLite delivers ~500 claims/sec at that size.\n5. **Server is a future optimization**: A persistent in-memory server would reduce per-claim latency from ~2ms to ~0.1ms by eliminating SQLite file I/O entirely. Premature for current scale — can be added transparently later.\n\n### 4.7 Comparison to br's Race Condition\n\n| Scenario | br (beads_rust) | bf (bead-forge) |\n|----------|-----------------|-----------------|\n| 20 workers claim simultaneously | 20 read same JSONL, all see same \"next\" bead, all write `in_progress` — last writer wins, 19 workers have phantom claims | 20 workers serialized by SQLite write lock — each BEGIN IMMEDIATE sees post-commit state, each gets a distinct bead |\n| Worker crashes mid-claim | N/A (no lock) | SQLite rolls back the incomplete transaction automatically on next open |\n| Stale claim (worker died) | Bead stays `in_progress` forever | Next claim invocation reclaims beads older than TTL |\n| Cross-workspace claiming | Not supported | `--any` locks all JSONL files in sorted order |\n\n---\n\n## Phase 4B: Extended Features\n\n### 4B.1 JSONL Rotation (`src/rotate.rs`)\n\nKeep `issues.jsonl` lean for fast claiming. With one entry per bead, the file grows only when new beads are created — but over a long project lifetime the accumulation of closed beads still grows the file. Rotation evicts closed beads into numbered archive files, keeping the active scan set small.\n\n```\n.beads/\n├── issues.jsonl          # active: open, in_progress, blocked, deferred + recently closed\n├── issues.jsonl.1        # archive: closed beads rotated out most recently\n├── issues.jsonl.2        # archive: older closed beads\n└── issues.jsonl.3        # archive: oldest\n```\n\n**Rotation rules**:\n- `bf rotate` moves closed beads older than `rotate_age_days` (default 30) from `issues.jsonl` into `issues.jsonl.1` via streaming rewrite (active file) + streaming append (archive file)\n- When `issues.jsonl.1` exceeds `rotate_max_size` (default 5MB), it becomes `issues.jsonl.2`, `.2` → `.3`, etc.\n- Maximum archive files: `rotate_max_archives` (default 5). Oldest archive is deleted.\n\n**Claim only reads the active file**. This keeps claim latency proportional to the number of active (non-closed) beads rather than total workspace history.\n\n**Reading archived beads**: `bf show <id>` searches the active file first, then archives. `bf list --all` includes archives. `bf log <id>` searches all files for the full history.\n\n**git integration**: `.beads/issues.jsonl` is committed to git. Archive files (`.jsonl.1`, `.2`, ...) are also committed — they're part of the project history. The `.beads/` directory is the bead store and should be version-controlled alongside the code.\n\n**Secret protection**: Before any write, bf scans bead fields for secret patterns (AWS keys, private keys, API tokens, passwords) using a built-in regex set similar to git-secrets. If a match is found:\n- `bf create` / `bf update` prints a warning and refuses the write\n- `bf commit-check` (git pre-commit hook) scans staged changes to `.beads/` for secrets\n- Patterns are configurable in `.beads/config.yaml`:\n\n```yaml\nsecret_protection:\n  enabled: true\n  # Additional patterns (matched against all string fields)\n  deny_patterns:\n    - \"-----BEGIN (RSA |EC |DSA )?PRIVATE KEY-----\"\n    - \"AKIA[0-9A-Z]{16}\"          # AWS access key\n    - \"sk-[a-zA-Z0-9]{32,}\"       # OpenAI/Anthropic API key\n  # Fields to exclude from scanning (e.g., known test values)\n  allowlist_fields: []\n```\n\nConfig:\n\n```yaml\nrotate_age_days: 30\nrotate_max_size_mb: 5\nrotate_max_archives: 5\n```\n\n### 4B.2 Batch Operations (`src/batch.rs`)\n\nExecute multiple operations atomically under a single SQLite `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` transaction. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_4f251c7a2fcd46029a248c5c","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t//! JSONL log rotation for closed beads.\n2\t//!\n3\t//! Moves closed beads older than `rotate_age_days` from issues.jsonl into\n4\t//! numbered archive files (issues.jsonl.1, .2, etc.). Uses streaming rewrite\n5\t//! of the active file and streaming append to archives for efficiency.\n6\t\n7\tuse crate::config::{load_config, load_metadata};\n8\tuse crate::model::Issue;\n9\tuse anyhow::{anyhow, Result};\n10\tuse chrono::{DateTime, Duration, Utc};\n11\tuse std::fs::File;\n12\tuse std::io::{BufRead, BufReader, BufWriter, Write};\n13\tuse std::path::{Path, PathBuf};\n14\tuse std::collections::HashSet;\n15\t\n16\t/// Result of a rotation operation.\n17\t#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]\n18\tpub struct RotateResult {\n19\t    /// Number of beads archived\n20\t    pub archived: usize,\n21\t    /// Number of beads remaining in active file\n22\t    pub remaining: usize,\n23\t    /// Path to the archive file created (if any)\n24\t    pub archive_path: Option<PathBuf>,\n25\t    /// Paths to archives deleted due to rotate_max_archives\n26\t    pub deleted_archives: Vec<PathBuf>,\n27\t}\n28\t\n29\t/// Rotation configuration.\n30\t#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n31\tpub struct RotateOptions {\n32\t    /// Days threshold for rotating closed beads\n33\t    pub age_days: u64,\n34\t    /// Maximum size of active JSONL in MB before considering rotation\n35\t    pub max_size_mb: Option<u64>,\n36\t    /// Maximum number of archive files to keep\n37\t    pub max_archives: usize,\n38\t    /// Dry run - show what would be done without making changes\n39\t    pub dry_run: bool,\n40\t}\n41\t\n42\timpl RotateOptions {\n43\t    pub fn from_config(age_days: u64, config: &crate::config::Config) -> Self {\n44\t        RotateOptions {\n45\t            age_days,\n46\t            max_size_mb: Some(config.rotate.rotate_max_size_mb),\n47\t            max_archives: config.rotate.rotate_max_archives,\n48\t            dry_run: false,\n49\t        }\n50\t    }\n51\t\n52\t    pub fn dry_run(mut self) -> Self {\n53\t        self.dry_run = true;\n54\t        self\n55\t    }\n56\t}\n57\t\n58\t/// Rotate closed beads from active JSONL to archive files.\n59\t///\n60\t/// # Algorithm\n61\t/// 1. Scan active JSONL and identify beads to archive (closed + age threshold)\n62\t/// 2. Determine next archive number (issues.jsonl.1, .2, etc.)\n63\t/// 3. Stream active file: keep active beads in memory, append archived beads to archive\n64\t/// 4. Rewrite active file with only active beads\n65\t/// 5. Delete oldest archives if exceeding rotate_max_archives\n66\t///\n67\t/// # Arguments\n68\t/// * `beads_dir` - Path to the .beads directory\n69\t/// * `options` - Rotation options\n70\t///\n71\t/// # Returns\n72\t/// * `Ok(RotateResult)` - Statistics about the rotation\n73\tpub fn rotate(beads_dir: &Path, options: &RotateOptions) -> Result<RotateResult> {\n74\t    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n75\t    let jsonl_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.jsonl_export);\n76\t\n77\t    if !jsonl_path.exists() {\n78\t        return Ok(RotateResult::default());\n79\t    }\n80\t\n81\t    let cutoff_time = Utc::now() - Duration::days(options.age_days as i64);\n82\t\n83\t    // Phase 1: Scan and categorize beads\n84\t    let mut active_beads = Vec::new();\n85\t    let mut archive_beads = Vec::new();\n86\t    let mut archived_ids = HashSet::new();\n87\t\n88\t    let file = File::open(&jsonl_path)?;\n89\t    let reader = BufReader::new(file);\n90\t\n91\t    for line in reader.lines() {\n92\t        let line = line?;\n93\t        if line.trim().is_empty() {\n94\t            continue;\n95\t        }\n96\t\n97\t        let issue: Issue = serde_json::from_str(&line)\n98\t            .map_err(|e| anyhow!(\"Failed to parse JSONL line: {}\", e))?;\n99\t\n100\t        if should_archive(&issue, &cutoff_time) {\n101\t            archive_beads.push(issue.clone());\n102\t            archived_ids.insert(issue.id);\n103\t        } else {\n104\t            active_beads.push(issue);\n105\t        }\n106\t    }\n107\t\n108\t    if archive_beads.is_empty() {\n109\t        return Ok(RotateResult {\n110\t            archived: 0,\n111\t            remaining: active_beads.len(),\n112\t            archive_path: None,\n113\t            deleted_archives: Vec::new(),\n114\t        });\n115\t    }\n116\t\n117\t    // Phase 2: Find next archive number\n118\t    let archive_num = find_next_archive_number(beads_dir, &metadata.jsonl_export)?;\n119\t    let archive_path = beads_dir.join(format!(\"{}.{}\", metadata.jsonl_export, archive_num));\n120\t\n121\t    if options.dry_run {\n122\t        return Ok(RotateResult {\n123\t            archived: archive_beads.len(),\n124\t            remaining: active_beads.len(),\n125\t            archive_path: Some(archive_path),\n126\t            deleted_archives: Vec::new(),\n127\t        });\n128\t    }\n129\t\n130\t    // Phase 3: Write archive file\n131\t    {\n132\t        let archive_file = File::create(&archive_path)?;\n133\t        let mut writer = BufWriter::new(archive_file);\n134\t\n135\t        for bead in &archive_beads {\n136\t            serde_json::to_writer(&mut writer, bead)?;\n137\t            writer.write_all(b\"\\n\")?;\n138\t        }\n139\t\n140\t        writer.flush()?;\n141\t    }\n142\t\n143\t    // Phase 4: Rewrite active file\n144\t    let temp_path = jsonl_path.with_extension(\"jsonl.tmp\");\n145\t    {\n146\t        let temp_file = File::create(&temp_path)?;\n147\t        let mut writer = BufWriter::new(temp_file);\n148\t\n149\t        for bead in &active_beads {\n150\t            serde_json::to_writer(&mut writer, bead)?;\n151\t            writer.write_all(b\"\\n\")?;\n152\t        }\n153\t\n154\t        writer.flush()?;\n155\t    }\n156\t\n157\t    // Atomic rename\n158\t    std::fs::rename(&temp_path, &jsonl_path)?;\n159\t\n160\t    // Phase 5: Delete old archives if exceeding max_archives\n161\t    let deleted_archives = cleanup_old_archives(\n162\t        beads_dir,\n163\t        &metadata.jsonl_export,\n164\t        options.max_archives,\n165\t    )?;\n166\t\n167\t    Ok(RotateResult {\n168\t        archived: archive_beads.len(),\n169\t        remaining: active_beads.len(),\n170\t        archive_path: Some(archive_path),\n171\t        deleted_archives,\n172\t    })\n173\t}\n174\t\n175\t/// Determine if a bead should be archived.\n176\t///\n177\t/// A bead is archived if:\n178\t/// - Status is Closed or Tombstone\n179\t/// - closed_at timestamp exists and is older than cutoff_time\n180\tfn should_archive(issue: &Issue, cutoff_time: &DateTime<Utc>) -> bool {\n181\t    match issue.status {\n182\t        crate::model::Status::Closed | crate::model::Status::Tombstone => {\n183\t            if let Some(closed_at) = issue.closed_at {\n184\t                return closed_at < *cutoff_time;\n185\t            }\n186\t            false\n187\t        }\n188\t        _ => false,\n189\t    }\n190\t}\n191\t\n192\t/// Find the next available archive number.\n193\t///\n194\t/// Scans for existing archive files (issues.jsonl.1, .2, etc.) and returns\n195\t/// the next number in sequence.\n196\tfn find_next_archive_number(beads_dir: &Path, base_name: &str) -> Result<usize> {\n197\t    let mut max_num = 0;\n198\t\n199\t    let entries = std::fs::read_dir(beads_dir)?;\n200\t    for entry in entries {\n201\t        let entry = entry?;\n202\t        let name = entry.file_name();\n203\t        let name_str = name.to_string_lossy();\n204\t\n205\t        if let Some(suffix) = name_str.strip_prefix(base_name) {\n206\t            if let Some(num_str) = suffix.strip_prefix('.') {\n207\t                if let Ok(num) = num_str.parse::<usize>() {\n208\t                    max_num = max_num.max(num);\n209\t                }\n210\t            }\n211\t        }\n212\t    }\n213\t\n214\t    Ok(max_num + 1)\n215\t}\n216\t\n217\t/// Delete old archives if we exceed max_archives.\n218\t///\n219\t/// Keeps only the most recent `max_archives` files.\n220\tfn cleanup_old_archives(\n221\t    beads_dir: &Path,\n222\t    base_name: &str,\n223\t    max_archives: usize,\n224\t) -> Result<Vec<PathBuf>> {\n225\t    let mut archives: Vec<(usize, PathBuf)> = Vec::new();\n226\t\n227\t    let entries = std::fs::read_dir(beads_dir)?;\n228\t    for entry in entries {\n229\t        let entry = entry?;\n230\t        let path = entry.path();\n231\t        let name = entry.file_name();\n232\t        let name_str = name.to_string_lossy();\n233\t\n234\t        if let Some(suffix) = name_str.strip_prefix(base_name) {\n235\t            if let Some(num_str) = suffix.strip_prefix('.') {\n236\t                if let Ok(num) = num_str.parse::<usize>() {\n237\t                    archives.push((num, path.clone()));\n238\t                }\n239\t            }\n240\t        }\n241\t    }\n242\t\n243\t    // Sort by archive number (oldest first)\n244\t    archives.sort_by_key(|(num, _)| *num);\n245\t\n246\t    let mut deleted = Vec::new();\n247\t    let num_to_delete = archives.len().saturating_sub(max_archives);\n248\t\n249\t    for (num, path) in Active JSONL file\n261\t/// 2. Archive files (highest number first - most recent)\n262\t///\n263\t/// Returns None if the bead is not found in any file.\n264\tpub fn find_bead_in_archives(beads_dir: &Path, bead_id: &str) ->
